@return The text of the input as a double.\n     */\n    private double validateCoordinateInput(InputTextView input) {\n        String inputText = input.getInputText();\n\n        // do not validate if the user is entering a negative coordinate\n        if (inputText.length() == 1 && inputText.charAt(0) == \'-\')\n            return -1;\n\n        try {\n            inputText = }\n        });\n    }\n\n    private void updateCoordinateViews(LatLng coordinates) {\n        mLatitudeView.setInputText(String.format(getResources().getConfiguration().locale, "%.6f", {@link InputTextView}. If the input is invalid a Toast will be displayed\n     * to the user and the previously entered character will be removed.\n     *\n     * @param input The {@link InputTextView} to validate.\n     * @return The text of the input as a double.\n     */\n    private double validateCoordinateInput(InputTextView input) {\n        String inputText = input.getInputText();\n\n        // do not validate if the user is entering a negative coordinate\n        if (inputText.length() == 1 && inputText.charAt(0) == \'-\')\n            return -1;\n\n        try {\n            inputText = inputText.replace(\',\', \'.\'); // some countries/keyboard use \',\' in place of \'.\'\n            return (double) Utils.asFloat(inputText, 0);\n        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {\n   }\n\n    private void updateCamera(LatLng loc) {\n        mMapFragment.updateCamera(loc);\n        updateCoordinateViews(loc);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Changes the color of the center crosshairs mLongitudeView.setInputText(String.format(getResources().getConfiguration().locale, "%.6f", coordinates.longitude));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Gets the coordinates currently entered in the latitude @Nullable\n    private LatLng getInputCoordinates() {\n        Double latitude = coordinateFromInput(mLatitudeView);\n        Double longitude = coordinateFromInput(mLongitudeView);\n\n        if mLongitudeView.setInputText(String.format(getResources().getConfiguration().locale, "%.6f", coordinates.longitude));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Gets the coordinates currently entered in the latitude @Nullable\n    private LatLng getInputCoordinates() {\n        Double latitude = coordinateFromInput(mLatitudeView);\n        Double longitude = coordinateFromInput(mLongitudeView);\n\n        if (latitude == null || longitude == null)\n            return null;\n\n        LatLng latLng = new LatLng(latitude, longitude);\n\n        return mMapFragment != null && !mMapFragment.isValid(latLng) ? sync with the map after a drag gesture settles\n                mMap.setOnCameraIdleListener(new GoogleMap.OnCameraIdleListener() {\n                    @Override\n                    public void        updateCoordinateViews(mMap.getCameraPosition().target);\n                    }\n                });\n\n                updateMap();\n            }\n\n            @Override\n            public void onLocationUpdate(android.location.Location location) {\n                if (mStopLocationUpdates) {\n                    mMapFragment.setLocationUpdatesEnabled(false);\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                updateCamera(new LatLng(location.getLatitude(), location.getLongitude()));\n                mMapFragment.setLocationUpdatesEnabled(false);\n      }\n        });\n\n        updateCoordinateViews(new LatLng(0.0, 0.0));\n    }\n\n    private void updateCamera(LatLng loc) {\n        mMapFragment.updateCamera(loc);\n updateCrosshairs(int mapType) {\n        boolean isDark = mapType == GoogleMap.MAP_TYPE_SATELLITE || mapType == GoogleMap.MAP_TYPE_HYBRID;\n        mCrosshairs.setColorFilter(isDark ? Typed coordinates only reached the map if the user pressed the "sync" button, so unsynced manual input was silently discarded on save.\n\n**Fix:**\n1. **Coordinates now come from the `TextView` widgets.** `verifyUserInput()` calls the new `getInputCoordinates()`, which parses both `InputTextView`s and returns `null` when the input is invalid.\n2. **Invalid input shows a dialog.** Instead of a transient toast plus a backspace hack, invalid coordinates trigger `AlertUtils.showError(..., R.string.msg_invalid_coordinate)` and block the save. The sync button uses the same path, so its behavior is consistent.\n3. **Validation before `LatLng` construction.** `LatLng` clamps latitude and wraps longitude, which made the old `isValid()`-after-construction check a no-op; range checks now run on the raw parsed doubles, so `200` is rejected rather than silently clamped to `90`.\n4. **Precision preserved.** Parsing uses `Double.parseDouble` rather than `Utils.asFloat`, avoiding ~1 m of float rounding error on 6-decimal coordinates. Empty input and a lone `-` are treated as invalid; `,` is still normalized to `.` for locales that use it.\n5. **Map drags still win when appropriate.** Since the text views are now the source of truth, a `OnCameraIdleListener` (guarded by a flag set on drag-up) re-syncs them after the camera settles, so fling inertia after a drag can\'t leave stale text behind.\n\nName/duplicate checks were moved ahead of coordinate validation so the user sees the most relevant error first; `isDuplicate()` only inspects the name, so the reordering is behavior-preserving.
